PowerDNS Recursor on `127.0.0.1:5353` with `i23.test` as an auth zone, forwarding to NSD, and the `svc`/`db` hosts export. dnsdist on `:53` (UDP and TCP) and `:853` (DoT, TLS 1.2+), using the reference's Lua policy: ANY and CHAOS are REFUSED, `blocked.a23.test` is sinkholed to NXDOMAIN, rate limiting is 50 QPS per IP, and the packet cache is kept.\n- **Accounts and access:** `birch` and `cedar` with UIDs/GIDs 1301/1302, locked passwords, and `/bin/bash`. Their `rec_control` sudo rules match the reference: birch may `wipe-cache` and `get`, cedar may only `get`. Service users `_dnsdist`, `nsd`, and `pdns` keep their reference UIDs and GIDs. Root is key-only over SSH.\n- **Paths:** `/bin/bash`, `/bin/false`, `/usr/sbin/nologin`, and `/usr/bin/rec_control` exist, since the reference has them.\n\n**Choices I made**\n- I wrote dnsdist's systemd unit myself, because the NixOS module can't express `reusePort` and always adds its own `setLocal`.\n- The firewall is enabled with 22, 53, and 853 open. The reference has no firewall, but clients see the same behavior.\n- SSH host keys are generated per boot, as they were on the reference, so I didn't copy any.\n\n**Known differences**\n- **RD=0 queries:** the reference answers non-recursive (`+norec`) queries with NOERROR. The replica returns REFUSED. This comes from the Recursor version: 4.6.0 on the reference, 5.4.6 in nixpkgs. I found no 5.x setting for it, and dnsdist 2.0.7 can clear RD but not set it, so I couldn't fix it in front.\n- **Versions:** dnsdist 2.0.7 (ref 1.6.1), NSD 4.12.0 (ref 4.3.9), PowerDNS Recursor 5.4.6 (ref 4.6.0). The dnsdist web API changed from one `webserver()` call to `setWebserverConfig()` plus `webserver()`. The credentials are the same.\n- **Not replicated:** the stock Ubuntu system accounts, snap, cron, unattended-upgrades, and cloud-init. Skeleton dotfiles for `birch` and `cedar` are also absent.\n- **Not verified:** the 50 QPS drop, the XFR grant to `198.51.100.7`, and ACL rejection of non-private sources. The config is the same, but I didn't exercise these.\n\nI briefly killed the reference VM by mistake during cleanup, then restarted it from its existing overlay disk. Both VMs are stopped now."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":342,"cache_read_input_tokens":185569,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":342,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"cb3d25b4-9ea5-4cad-b498-d5e147ba1b08","uuid":"77d7203e-aba0-4e6e-b2c3-8e0bb66644f4","timestamp":"2026-11-04T06:26:16.924Z","request_id":"req_011CghQqETe8sKJ4h5oRZXMy"}
